| 7 | `VIRTUAL_OD_EN` | RW | 0x0 a| Enable virtual open drain:

0 - disabled

1 - enabled
| 6 | `OD_EN` | RW | 0x0 a| Enable Open-Drain:

0 - disabled

1 - enabled
| 5 | `IO_INVERSION` | RW | 0x0 a| Invert I/O signal:

0 - signals pass-through

1 - signals are inverted
| 4 | `PULL_EN` | RW | 0x0 a| Enable Pull:

0 - disabled

1 - enabled
| 3 | `PULL_DIR` | RW | 0x0 a| Direction of the pull:

0 - Pull down

1 - Pull up
| 2 | `KEEPER_EN` | RW | 0x0 a| Enable the High-Keeper:

0 - disabled

1 - enabled
| 1 | `SCHMITT_EN` | RW | 0x0 a| Enable the Schmitt Trigger:

0 - disabled

1 - enabled
| 0 | `INPUT_ENABLE` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable input:

0 - enabled

1 - disabled
|===

If both SCL and SDA stay High for this many clock cycles during a TE0/TE1 error-induced HDR mode, the Target exits HDR mode and returns to Idle. Configure based on System Clock to achieve 60us.

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]

| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31 | `BUS_ENABLE` | RW | 0x0 a| Host Controller Bus Enable
| 30 | `RESUME` | RW | 0x0 a| Host Controller Resume:

0 - Controller is running

1 - Controller is suspended

Write 1 to resume Controller operations.
| 29 | `ABORT` | RW | 0x0 a| Host Controller Abort when set to 1
| 28:13 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 12 | `HALT_ON_CMD_SEQ_TIMEOUT` | RW | 0x0 a| Halt on Command Sequence Timeout when set to 1
| 11:9 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 8 | `HOT_JOIN_CTRL` | RW | 0x0 a| Hot-Join ACK/NACK Control:

0 - ACK Hot-Join request

1 - NACK Hot-Join request and send Broadcast CCC to disable Hot-Join
| 7 | `I2C_DEV_PRESENT` | RW | 0x0 a| I2C Device Present on Bus:

0 - pure I3C bus

1 - legacy I2C devices on the bus
| 6 | `MODE_SELECTOR` | R | 0x1 a| DMA/PIO Mode Selector:

0 - DMA

1 - PIO
| 5 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 4 | `DATA_BYTE_ORDER_MODE` | R | 0x0 a| Data Byte Ordering Mode:

0 - Little Endian

1 - Big Endian
| 3 | `AUTOCMD_DATA_RPT` | R | 0x0 a| Auto-Command Data Report:

0 - coalesced reporting

1 - separated reporting
| 2:1 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 0 | `IBA_INCLUDE` | RW | 0x0 a| Include I3C Broadcast Address:

0 - skips I3C Broadcast Address for private transfers

1 - includes I3C Broadcast Address for private transfers
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-3-PIOControl-IBI_PORT]
=== i3c_csr[3].PIOControl.IBI_PORT

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:0 | `IBI_DATA` | R | - a| Data Read from IBI Data Buffer.

The IBI Port is mapped to the IBI Data Queue.
IBI Data is always aligned to a 4-byte boundary and then put into the IBI Queue.
If the incoming data is not aligned to a 4-byte boundary,
then there will be extra (unused) bytes at the end of the transferred IBI data.
This can be determined from the value of field DATA_LENGTH in the IBI Status Descriptor.
|===

=== i3c_csr[3].PIOControl.PIO_CONTROL

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:3 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 2 | `ABORT` | RW | 0x0 a| Stop current command descriptor execution forcefully and hold remaining commands.
1 - Request PIO Abort,
0 - Resume PIO execution
| 1 | `RS` | RW | 0x0 a| Run/Stop execution of enqueued commands.
When set to 0, it holds execution of enqueued commands and runs current command to completion.
1 - PIO Queue start request,
0 - PIO Queue stop request.
| 0 | `ENABLE` | RW | 0x1 a| Enables PIO queues. When disabled, SW may not read from/write to PIO queues.
1 - PIO queue enable request,
0 - PIO queue disable request
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-3-I3C_EC-SecFwRecoveryIf-PROT_CAP_2]

| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:16 | `AGENT_CAPS` | RW | 0x0 a| Agent capabilities:

* bit 0: Identification (DEVICE_ID structure)

* bit 1: Forced Recovery (From RESET)

* bit 2: Mgmt reset (From RESET)

* bit 3: Device Reset (From RESET)

* bit 4: Device status (DEVICE_STATUS)

* bit 5: Recovery memory access (INDIRECT_CTRL)

* bit 6: Local C-image support

* bit 7: Push C-image support

* bit 8: Interface isolation

* bit 9: Hardware status

* bit 10: Vendor command

* bit 11: Flashless boot (From RESET)

* bit 12: FIFO CMS support (INDIRECT_FIFO_CTRL)

* bits 13-15: Reserved
| 15:0 | `REC_PROT_VERSION` | RW | 0x0 a| * Byte 0: Major version number = 0x1

* Byte 1: Minor version number = 0x1
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-3-I3C_EC-SecFwRecoveryIf-DEVICE_STATUS_0]
=== i3c_csr[3].I3C_EC.SecFwRecoveryIf.DEVICE_STATUS_0

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:16 | `REC_REASON_CODE` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: No Boot Failure detected (BFNF)

* 0x1: Generic hardware error (BFGHWE)

* 0x2: Generic hardware soft error (BFGSE) - soft error may be recoverable

* 0x3: Self-test failure (BFSTF) (e.g., RSA self test failure, FIPs self test failure,, etc.)

* 0x4: Corrupted/missing critical data (BFCD)

* 0x5: Missing/corrupt key manifest (BFKMMC)

* 0x6: Authentication Failure on key manifest (BFKMAF)

* 0x7: Anti-rollback failure on key manifest (BFKIAR)

* 0x8: Missing/corrupt boot loader (first mutable code) firmware image (BFFIMC)

* 0x9: Authentication failure on boot loader ( 1st mutable code) firmware image (BFFIAF)

* 0xA: Anti-rollback failure boot loader (1st mutable code) firmware image (BFFIAR)

* 0xB: Missing/corrupt main/management firmware image (BFMFMC)

* 0xC: Authentication Failure main/management firmware image (BFMFAF)

* 0xD: Anti-rollback Failure main/management firmware image (BFMFAR)

* 0xE: Missing/corrupt recovery firmware (BFRFMC)

* 0xF: Authentication Failure recovery firmware (BFRFAF)

* 0x10: Anti-rollback Failure on recovery firmware (BFRFAR)

* 0x11: Forced Recovery (FR)

* 0x12: Flashless/Streaming Boot (FSB)

* 0x13-0x7F: Reserved

* 0x80-0xFF: Vendor Unique Boot Failure Codes
| 15:8 | `PROT_ERROR` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: No Protocol Error

* 0x1: Unsupported/Write Command - command is not support or a write to a RO command

* 0x2: Unsupported Parameter

* 0x3: Length write error (length of write command is incorrect)

* 0x4: CRC Error (if supported)

* 0x5-0xFE: Reserved

* 0xFF: General Protocol Error - catch all unclassified errors
| 7:0 | `DEV_STATUS` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: Status Pending (Recover Reason Code not populated)

* 0x1: Device healthy (Recover Reason Code not populated)

* 0x2: Device Error (“soft” error or other error state) - (Recover Reason Code not populated)

* 0x3: Recovery mode - ready to accept recovery image - (Recover Reason Code populated)

* 0x4: Recovery Pending (waiting for activation) - (Recover Reason Code populated)

* 0x5: Running Recovery Image ( Recover Reason Code not populated)

* 0x6-0xD: Reserved

* 0xE: Boot Failure (Recover Reason Code populated)

* 0xF: Fatal Error (Recover Reason Code not populated)

* 0x10-FF:Reserved
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-3-I3C_EC-SecFwRecoveryIf-DEVICE_STATUS_1]

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:25 | `VENDOR_STATUS` | RW | 0x0 a| -
| 24:16 | `VENDOR_STATUS_LENGTH` | RW | 0x0 a| 0-248: Length in bytes of just VENDOR_STATUS. Zero indicates no vendor status and zero additional bytes.
| 15:0 | `HEARTBEAT` | RW | 0x0 a| 0-4095: Incrementing number (counter wraps)
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-3-I3C_EC-SecFwRecoveryIf-DEVICE_RESET]
=== i3c_csr[3].I3C_EC.SecFwRecoveryIf.DEVICE_RESET

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:24 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 23:16 | `IF_CTRL` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: Disable Interface mastering

* 0x1: Enable Interface mastering
| 15:8 | `FORCED_RECOVERY` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: No forced recovery

* 0x01-0xD: Reserved

* 0xE: Enter flashless boot mode on next platform reset

* 0xF: Enter recovery mode on next platform reset

* 0x10-FF: Reserved
| 7:0 | `RESET_CTRL` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: No reset

* 0x1: Reset Device (PCIe Fundamental Reset or equivalent. This is likely bus disruptive)

* 0x2: Reset Management. This reset will reset the management subsystem. If supported, this reset MUST not be bus disruptive (cause re-enumeration)

* 0x3-FF: Reserved
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-3-I3C_EC-SecFwRecoveryIf-RECOVERY_CTRL]

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]

| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:24 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 23:16 | `ACTIVATE_REC_IMG` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: do not activate recovery image - after activation device will report this code.

* 0xF: Activate recovery image

* 0x10-FF-reserved
| 15:8 | `REC_IMG_SEL` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: No operation

* 0x1: Use Recovery Image from memory window (CMS)

* 0x2: Use Recovery Image stored on device (C-image)

* 0x3-FF: reserved
| 7:0 | `CMS` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0-255: Selects a component memory space where the recovery image is. 0 is the default

| 7:4 | `REC_IMG_INDEX` | RW | 0x0 a| -
| 3:0 | `DEV_REC_STATUS` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: Not in recovery mode

* 0x1: Awaiting recovery image

* 0x2: Booting recovery image

* 0x3: Recovery successful

| 23:16 | `CTEMP` | RW | 0x0 a| Current temperatureof device in degrees Celsius: Compatible with NVMe-MI command code 0 offset 3.

* 0x00-0x7e: 0 to 126 C

* 0x7f: 127 C or higher

* 0x80: no temperature data, or data is older than 5 seconds

* 0x81: temperature sensor failure

* 0x82-0x83: reserved

* 0xc4: -60 C or lower

* 0xc5-0xff: -59 to -1 C (in two's complement)

|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:30 | `STBY_CR_ENABLE_INIT` | RW | 0x0 a| Enables or disables the Secondary Controller:

2'b00 - DISABLED: Secondary Controller is disabled.

2'b01 - ACM_INIT: Secondary Controller is enabled,
but Host Controller initializes in Active Controller mode.

2'b10 - SCM_RUNNING: Secondary Controller operation is enabled,
Host Controller initializes in Standby Controller mode.

2'b11 - SCM_HOT_JOIN: Secondary Controller operation is enabled,
Host Controller conditionally becomes a Hot-Joining Device
to receive its Dynamic Address before operating in Standby Controller mode.
| 29:21 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 20 | `RSTACT_DEFBYTE_02` | RW | 0x0 a| Controls whether I3C Secondary Controller Logic supports RSTACT CCC with
Defining Byte 0x02.

1'b0: NOT_SUPPORTED: Do not ACK Defining Byte 0x02

1'b1: HANDLE_INTR: Support Defining Byte 0x02
| 19:16 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 15 | `DAA_ENTDAA_ENABLE` | RW | 0x0 a| Indicates ENTDAA method is enabled.

1'b0: DISABLED: will not respond

1'b1: ENABLED: will respond
| 14 | `DAA_SETDASA_ENABLE` | RW | 0x0 a| Indicates SETDASA method is enabled.

1'b0: DISABLED: will not respond

1'b1: ENABLED: will respond
| 13 | `DAA_SETAASA_ENABLE` | RW | 0x0 a| Indicates SETAASA method is enabled.

1'b0: DISABLED: will not respond

1'b1: ENABLED: will respond
| 12 | `TARGET_XACT_ENABLE` | RW | 0x1 a| Indicates whether Read-Type/Write-Type transaction servicing is enabled, via
an I3C Target Transaction Interface to software (Section 6.17.3).

1'b0: DISABLED: not available

1'b1: ENABLED: available for software
| 11 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 10:8 | `BAST_CCC_IBI_RING` | RW | 0x0 a| Indicates which Ring Bundle will be used to capture Broadcast CCC data sent by the Active Controller.
The Ring Bundle must be configured and enabled, and its IBI Ring Pair must also be initialized and ready to receive data.
| 7:6 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 5 | `CR_REQUEST_SEND` | W | 0x0 a| Write of 1'b1 to this field shall instruct the Secondary Controller Logic
to attempt to send a Controller Role Request to the I3C Bus.
| 4 | `HANDOFF_DEEP_SLEEP` | RW | 0x0 a| If this field has a value of 1'b1, then the Secondary Controller Logic shall
report a return from Deep Sleep state to the Active Controller.
Writing 1'b1 to this bit is sticky. This field shall automatically clear to 1'b0
after accepting the Controller Role and transitioning to Active Controller mode.
| 3 | `PRIME_ACCEPT_GETACCCR` | RW | 0x0 a| -
| 2 | `ACR_FSM_OP_SELECT` | RW | 0x0 a| -
| 1 | `HANDOFF_DELAY_NACK` | RW | 0x0 a| -
| 0 | `PENDING_RX_NACK` | RW | 0x0 a| -

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-3-I3C_EC-StdbyCtrlMode-STBY_CR_VIRTUAL_DEVICE_CHAR]
=== i3c_csr[3].I3C_EC.StdbyCtrlMode.STBY_CR_VIRTUAL_DEVICE_CHAR

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:29 | `BCR_FIXED` | RW | 0x1 a| Bus Characteristics, Fixed Part.

Reset value is set to 3'b001, because this device is an I3C Target,
which supports extended capabilities.

This field is Read-Only while the STBY_CR_ENABLE_INIT field in register
STBY_CR_CONTROL is not zero.
| 28:24 | `BCR_VAR` | RW | 0x10 a| Bus Characteristics, Variable Part.

Reset value is set to 5'b10000, because this device:


- [bit4] is a Virtual Target

- [bit3] is not Offline Capable

- [bit2] does not apply due to bit1

- [bit1] is not capable of IBI requests

- [bit0] has no speed limitation

This field is Read-Only while the STBY_CR_ENABLE_INIT field in register
STBY_CR_CONTROL is not zero.
| 23:16 | `DCR` | RW | 0xBD a| Device Characteristics Register. Value represents an OCP Recovery Device.

This field is Read-Only while the STBY_CR_ENABLE_INIT field in register
STBY_CR_CONTROL is not zero.
| 15:1 | `PID_HI` | RW | 0x7FFF a| High part of the 48-bit Target Device Provisioned ID.

This field is Read-Only while the STBY_CR_ENABLE_INIT field in register
STBY_CR_CONTROL is not zero.
| 0 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-3-I3C_EC-StdbyCtrlMode-STBY_CR_CCC_CONFIG_RSTACT_PARAMS]
=== i3c_csr[3].I3C_EC.StdbyCtrlMode.STBY_CR_CCC_CONFIG_RSTACT_PARAMS

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31 | `RESET_DYNAMIC_ADDR` | RW | 0x1 a| If set to 1'b1, then the Secondary Controller Logic must clear its Dynamic
Address in register STBY_CR_DEVICE_ADDR after receiving
a Target Reset Pattern that followed a Broadcast or Direct SET RSTACT CCC sent
to the Dynamic Address, with Defining Byte 0x01 or 0x02.
Requires support for Dynamic Address Assignment with at least one supported
method, such as the ENTDAA CCC, with field DAA_ENTDAA_ENABLE set to 1'b1 in
register STBY_CR_CONTROL.
If field ACR_FSM_OP_SELECT in register STBY_CR_CONTROL is set to 1'b1, then
this field shall be cleared (i.e., readiness to accept the Controller Role
shall be revoked) with this Target Reset Pattern.
| 30:24 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 23:16 | `RESET_TIME_TARGET` | RW | 0x0 a| For Direct GET CCC, this field is returned for Defining Byte 0x82.
| 15:8 | `RESET_TIME_PERIPHERAL` | RW | 0x0 a| For Direct GET CCC, this field is returned for Defining Byte 0x81.
| 7:0 | `RST_ACTION` | R | 0x1 a| Contains the Defining Byte received with the last Direct SET CCC sent by the Active Controller. If not armed (START or RSTACT with unsupported defining byte) set to spec default 0x1.
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-3-I3C_EC-TTI-CONTROL]
=== i3c_csr[3].I3C_EC.TTI.CONTROL

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:16 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 15:13 | `IBI_RETRY_NUM` | RW | 0x0 a| Number of times the Target Device will try to request an IBI before giving up.

Values:

0x0 - Device will never retry.

0x1-0x6 - Device will retry this many times.

0x7 - Device will retry indefinitely until the Active Controller sets
DISINT bit in the DISEC command.
| 12 | `IBI_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable the IBI queue servicing.

Values:

0x0 - Device will not service the IBI queue.

0x1 - Device will send IBI requests onto the bus, if possible.
| 11 | `CRR_EN` | RW | 0x0 a| Enable Controller Role Request.

Values:

0x0 - Device is allowed to perform Controller Role Request.

0x1 - Device is not allowed to perform Controller Role Request.
| 10 | `HJ_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable Hot-Join capability.

Values:

0x0 - Device is allowed to attempt Hot-Join.

0x1 - Device is not allowed to attempt Hot-Join.
| 9:0 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-3-I3C_EC-TTI-STATUS]
=== i3c_csr[3].I3C_EC.TTI.STATUS

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:15 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 14:12 | `LAST_IBI_STATUS` | R | 0x0 a| Status of last IBI. Should be read after IBI_DONE interrupt.

Values:

000 - Success: IBI was transmitted and ACK'd by the Active Controller.
001 - FailureNack: Active Controller NACK'd the IBI before any data was sent.
The device will retry sending the IBI as long as permitted by the retry counter.
010 - FailurePartialData: Active Controller NACK'd the IBI after data was
partially sent. The remaining part of data in the IBI queue is considered
corrupted and will be discarded.
011 - FailureRetry: IBI could not be serviced due to reached retry count.
100 - FailureArbitration:: IBI could not be serviced as arbitration was lost
during the address header. The device will retry sending the IBI as long as
permitted by the retry counter.
| 11:9 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 8 | `PROTOCOL_ERROR` | R | 0x0 a| Protocol error occurred in the past. This field can only be reset
by the Controller, if it issues the GETSTATUS CCC.

Values:

0 - no error occurred

1 - generic protocol error occurred in the past. It will be set until reception
of the next GETSTATUS command.
| 7:0 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-3-I3C_EC-TTI-INTERRUPT_STATUS]
=== i3c_csr[3].I3C_EC.TTI.INTERRUPT_STATUS

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31 | `TRANSFER_ERR_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| NOT IMPLEMENTED. Bus error occurred. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 30:27 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 26 | `TX_DESC_COMPLETE` | RW | 0x0 a| Read Transaction on the I3C Bus completed. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 25 | `TRANSFER_ABORT_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| NOT IMPLEMENTED. Bus aborted transaction. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 24:21 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 20 | `PENDING_IBI` | R | 0x0 a| At least one IBI is pending. Read-only, hardware-driven level status: it tracks the live IBI-pending condition and cannot be cleared by software (clears automatically when no IBI is pending).
| 19:16 | `PENDING_INTERRUPT` | RW | 0x0 a| Contains the interrupt number of any pending interrupt, or 0 if no interrupts are pending. This encoding allows for up to 15 numbered interrupts. If more than one interrupt is set, then the highest priority interrupt shall be returned. Read-only, hardware-driven level status: it cannot be cleared by software and updates as pending conditions change.
| 15:14 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 13 | `IBI_DONE` | RW | 0x0 a| IBI is done, check LAST_IBI_STATUS for result. Clear: auto-cleared by hardware when software reads STATUS.LAST_IBI_STATUS; writing 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS also clears it.
| 12 | `IBI_THLD_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| TTI IBI Buffer Threshold Status, the Target Controller shall set this bit to 1 when the number of available entries in the TTI IBI Queue is >= the value defined in `TTI_IBI_THLD`. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 11 | `RX_DESC_THLD_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| TTI RX Descriptor Buffer Threshold Status, the Target Controller shall set this bit to 1 when the number of available entries in the TTI RX Descriptor Queue is >= the value defined in `TTI_RX_DESC_THLD`. Clear: auto-cleared by hardware when software reads the RX Descriptor Queue; writing 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS also clears it.
| 10 | `TX_DESC_THLD_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| TTI TX Descriptor Buffer Threshold Status, the Target Controller shall set this bit to 1 when the number of available entries in the TTI TX Descriptor Queue is >= the value defined in `TTI_TX_DESC_THLD`. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 9 | `RX_DATA_THLD_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| TTI RX Data Buffer Threshold Status, the Target Controller shall set this bit to 1 when the number of entries in the TTI RX Data Queue is >= the value defined in `TTI_RX_DATA_THLD`. Clear: auto-cleared by hardware when software reads the RX Data Queue; writing 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS also clears it.
| 8 | `TX_DATA_THLD_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| TTI TX Data Buffer Threshold Status, the Target Controller shall set this bit to 1 when the number of available entries in the TTI TX Data Queue is >= the value defined in `TTI_TX_DATA_THLD`. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 7:4 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 3 | `TX_DESC_TIMEOUT` | RW | 0x0 a| NOT IMPLEMENTED. Pending Read was NACK’ed, because the `TX_DESC_STAT` event was not handled in time. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 2 | `RX_DESC_TIMEOUT` | RW | 0x0 a| NOT IMPLEMENTED. Pending Write was NACK’ed, because the `RX_DESC_STAT` event was not handled in time. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 1 | `TX_DESC_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| There is a pending Read Transaction on the I3C Bus. Software should write data to the TX Descriptor Queue and the TX Data Queue. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear — writing to the TX queues does not clear it).
| 0 | `RX_DESC_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| There is a pending Write Transaction. Software should read data from the RX Descriptor Queue and the RX Data Queue. Clear: auto-cleared by hardware when software reads the RX Descriptor Queue; writing 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS also clears it.
|===

| 12 | `RI_INDIRECT_FIFO_OVERFLOW_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable Recovery Interface INDIRECT FIFO overflow error detection and transition to Error state. Set to 0 to disable (overflow is still recorded in interrupt status).
| 11 | `RI_RX_FIFO_OVERFLOW_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable Recovery Interface RX FIFO overflow error detection and transition to Error state. Set to 0 to disable (overflow is still recorded in interrupt status).
| 10 | `RI_UNSUPPORTED_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable Recovery Interface unsupported command error detection. Set to 0 to disable.
| 9 | `RI_READONLY_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable Recovery Interface write-to-read-only error detection. Set to 0 to disable.
| 8 | `RI_LENGTH_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable Recovery Interface length error detection. Set to 0 to disable.
| 7 | `RI_PEC_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable Recovery Interface PEC/CRC error detection. Set to 0 to disable.
| 6 | `FRAMING_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable framing error detection. Set to 0 to disable.
| 5 | `TE5_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable TE5 error detection. Set to 0 to disable.
| 4 | `TE4_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable TE4 error detection. Set to 0 to disable.
| 3 | `TE3_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable TE3 error detection. Set to 0 to disable.
| 2 | `TE2_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable TE2 error detection. Set to 0 to disable.
| 1 | `TE1_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable TE1 error detection. Set to 0 to disable.
| 0 | `TE0_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable TE0 error detection. Set to 0 to disable.

0 - Pull down

1 - Pull up
| 2 | `KEEPER_EN` | RW | 0x0 a| Enable the High-Keeper:

0 - disabled

1 - enabled
| 1 | `SCHMITT_EN` | RW | 0x0 a| Enable the Schmitt Trigger:

0 - disabled

1 - enabled
| 0 | `INPUT_ENABLE` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable input:

If both SCL and SDA stay High for this many clock cycles during a TE0/TE1 error-induced HDR mode, the Target exits HDR mode and returns to Idle. Configure based on System Clock to achieve 60us.

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-4-I3CBase-HC_CONTROL]
=== i3c_csr[4].I3CBase.HC_CONTROL

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31 | `BUS_ENABLE` | RW | 0x0 a| Host Controller Bus Enable
| 30 | `RESUME` | RW | 0x0 a| Host Controller Resume:

0 - Controller is running

1 - Controller is suspended

Write 1 to resume Controller operations.
| 29 | `ABORT` | RW | 0x0 a| Host Controller Abort when set to 1
| 28:13 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 12 | `HALT_ON_CMD_SEQ_TIMEOUT` | RW | 0x0 a| Halt on Command Sequence Timeout when set to 1
| 11:9 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 8 | `HOT_JOIN_CTRL` | RW | 0x0 a| Hot-Join ACK/NACK Control:

0 - ACK Hot-Join request

1 - NACK Hot-Join request and send Broadcast CCC to disable Hot-Join
| 7 | `I2C_DEV_PRESENT` | RW | 0x0 a| I2C Device Present on Bus:

0 - pure I3C bus

1 - legacy I2C devices on the bus
| 6 | `MODE_SELECTOR` | R | 0x1 a| DMA/PIO Mode Selector:

0 - DMA

1 - PIO
| 5 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 4 | `DATA_BYTE_ORDER_MODE` | R | 0x0 a| Data Byte Ordering Mode:

0 - Little Endian

1 - Big Endian
| 3 | `AUTOCMD_DATA_RPT` | R | 0x0 a| Auto-Command Data Report:

0 - coalesced reporting

1 - separated reporting
| 2:1 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 0 | `IBA_INCLUDE` | RW | 0x0 a| Include I3C Broadcast Address:

0 - skips I3C Broadcast Address for private transfers

1 - includes I3C Broadcast Address for private transfers
|===

0 - not supported

1 - supported

1 - supported
| 5 | `STANDBY_CR_CAP` | R | 0x0 a| Switching from active to standby mode:

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-4-PIOControl-IBI_PORT]
=== i3c_csr[4].PIOControl.IBI_PORT

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:0 | `IBI_DATA` | R | - a| Data Read from IBI Data Buffer.

The IBI Port is mapped to the IBI Data Queue.
IBI Data is always aligned to a 4-byte boundary and then put into the IBI Queue.
If the incoming data is not aligned to a 4-byte boundary,
then there will be extra (unused) bytes at the end of the transferred IBI data.
This can be determined from the value of field DATA_LENGTH in the IBI Status Descriptor.
|===

| 5 | `TRANSFER_ABORT_FORCE` | W | 0x0 a| Force transfer aborted
| 4 | `RESP_READY_FORCE` | W | 0x0 a| Force response queue interrupt
| 3 | `CMD_QUEUE_READY_FORCE` | W | 0x0 a| Force command queue interrupt
| 2 | `IBI_THLD_FORCE` | W | 0x0 a| Force IBI queue interrupt
| 1 | `RX_THLD_FORCE` | W | 0x0 a| Force RX queue interrupt
| 0 | `TX_THLD_FORCE` | W | 0x0 a| Force TX queue interrupt
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-4-PIOControl-PIO_CONTROL]
=== i3c_csr[4].PIOControl.PIO_CONTROL

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:3 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 2 | `ABORT` | RW | 0x0 a| Stop current command descriptor execution forcefully and hold remaining commands.
1 - Request PIO Abort,
0 - Resume PIO execution
| 1 | `RS` | RW | 0x0 a| Run/Stop execution of enqueued commands.
When set to 0, it holds execution of enqueued commands and runs current command to completion.
1 - PIO Queue start request,
0 - PIO Queue stop request.
| 0 | `ENABLE` | RW | 0x1 a| Enables PIO queues. When disabled, SW may not read from/write to PIO queues.
1 - PIO queue enable request,
0 - PIO queue disable request
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-4-I3C_EC-SecFwRecoveryIf-PROT_CAP_2]
=== i3c_csr[4].I3C_EC.SecFwRecoveryIf.PROT_CAP_2

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:16 | `AGENT_CAPS` | RW | 0x0 a| Agent capabilities:

* bit 0: Identification (DEVICE_ID structure)

* bit 1: Forced Recovery (From RESET)

* bit 2: Mgmt reset (From RESET)

* bit 3: Device Reset (From RESET)

* bit 4: Device status (DEVICE_STATUS)

* bit 5: Recovery memory access (INDIRECT_CTRL)

* bit 6: Local C-image support

* bit 7: Push C-image support

* bit 8: Interface isolation

* bit 9: Hardware status

* bit 10: Vendor command

* bit 11: Flashless boot (From RESET)

* bit 12: FIFO CMS support (INDIRECT_FIFO_CTRL)

* bits 13-15: Reserved
| 15:0 | `REC_PROT_VERSION` | RW | 0x0 a| * Byte 0: Major version number = 0x1

* Byte 1: Minor version number = 0x1
|===

| 15:8 | `MAX_RESP_TIME` | RW | 0x0 a| 0-255: Maximum response time in 2^x microseconds(us).
| 7:0 | `NUM_OF_CMS_REGIONS` | RW | 0x0 a| 0-255: The total number of component memory space (CMS) regions a device supports. This number includes any logging, code and vendor defined regions

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-4-I3C_EC-SecFwRecoveryIf-DEVICE_STATUS_0]
=== i3c_csr[4].I3C_EC.SecFwRecoveryIf.DEVICE_STATUS_0

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:16 | `REC_REASON_CODE` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: No Boot Failure detected (BFNF)

* 0x1: Generic hardware error (BFGHWE)

* 0x2: Generic hardware soft error (BFGSE) - soft error may be recoverable

* 0x3: Self-test failure (BFSTF) (e.g., RSA self test failure, FIPs self test failure,, etc.)

* 0x4: Corrupted/missing critical data (BFCD)

* 0x5: Missing/corrupt key manifest (BFKMMC)

* 0x6: Authentication Failure on key manifest (BFKMAF)

* 0x7: Anti-rollback failure on key manifest (BFKIAR)

* 0x8: Missing/corrupt boot loader (first mutable code) firmware image (BFFIMC)

* 0x9: Authentication failure on boot loader ( 1st mutable code) firmware image (BFFIAF)

* 0xA: Anti-rollback failure boot loader (1st mutable code) firmware image (BFFIAR)

* 0xB: Missing/corrupt main/management firmware image (BFMFMC)

* 0xC: Authentication Failure main/management firmware image (BFMFAF)

* 0xD: Anti-rollback Failure main/management firmware image (BFMFAR)

* 0xE: Missing/corrupt recovery firmware (BFRFMC)

* 0xF: Authentication Failure recovery firmware (BFRFAF)

* 0x10: Anti-rollback Failure on recovery firmware (BFRFAR)

* 0x11: Forced Recovery (FR)

* 0x12: Flashless/Streaming Boot (FSB)

* 0x13-0x7F: Reserved

* 0x80-0xFF: Vendor Unique Boot Failure Codes
| 15:8 | `PROT_ERROR` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: No Protocol Error

* 0x1: Unsupported/Write Command - command is not support or a write to a RO command

* 0x2: Unsupported Parameter

* 0x3: Length write error (length of write command is incorrect)

* 0x4: CRC Error (if supported)

* 0x5-0xFE: Reserved

* 0xFF: General Protocol Error - catch all unclassified errors
| 7:0 | `DEV_STATUS` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: Status Pending (Recover Reason Code not populated)

* 0x1: Device healthy (Recover Reason Code not populated)

* 0x2: Device Error (“soft” error or other error state) - (Recover Reason Code not populated)

* 0x3: Recovery mode - ready to accept recovery image - (Recover Reason Code populated)

* 0x4: Recovery Pending (waiting for activation) - (Recover Reason Code populated)

* 0x5: Running Recovery Image ( Recover Reason Code not populated)

* 0x6-0xD: Reserved

* 0xE: Boot Failure (Recover Reason Code populated)

* 0xF: Fatal Error (Recover Reason Code not populated)

* 0x10-FF:Reserved
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-4-I3C_EC-SecFwRecoveryIf-DEVICE_STATUS_1]

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:25 | `VENDOR_STATUS` | RW | 0x0 a| -
| 24:16 | `VENDOR_STATUS_LENGTH` | RW | 0x0 a| 0-248: Length in bytes of just VENDOR_STATUS. Zero indicates no vendor status and zero additional bytes.
| 15:0 | `HEARTBEAT` | RW | 0x0 a| 0-4095: Incrementing number (counter wraps)
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-4-I3C_EC-SecFwRecoveryIf-DEVICE_RESET]
=== i3c_csr[4].I3C_EC.SecFwRecoveryIf.DEVICE_RESET

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:24 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 23:16 | `IF_CTRL` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: Disable Interface mastering

* 0x1: Enable Interface mastering
| 15:8 | `FORCED_RECOVERY` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: No forced recovery

* 0x01-0xD: Reserved

* 0xE: Enter flashless boot mode on next platform reset

* 0xF: Enter recovery mode on next platform reset

* 0x10-FF: Reserved
| 7:0 | `RESET_CTRL` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: No reset

* 0x1: Reset Device (PCIe Fundamental Reset or equivalent. This is likely bus disruptive)

* 0x2: Reset Management. This reset will reset the management subsystem. If supported, this reset MUST not be bus disruptive (cause re-enumeration)

* 0x3-FF: Reserved
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-4-I3C_EC-SecFwRecoveryIf-RECOVERY_CTRL]
=== i3c_csr[4].I3C_EC.SecFwRecoveryIf.RECOVERY_CTRL

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:24 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 23:16 | `ACTIVATE_REC_IMG` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: do not activate recovery image - after activation device will report this code.

* 0xF: Activate recovery image

* 0x10-FF-reserved
| 15:8 | `REC_IMG_SEL` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: No operation

* 0x1: Use Recovery Image from memory window (CMS)

* 0x2: Use Recovery Image stored on device (C-image)

* 0x3-FF: reserved
| 7:0 | `CMS` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0-255: Selects a component memory space where the recovery image is. 0 is the default
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-4-I3C_EC-SecFwRecoveryIf-RECOVERY_STATUS]
=== i3c_csr[4].I3C_EC.SecFwRecoveryIf.RECOVERY_STATUS

| 31:16 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 15:8 | `VENDOR_SPECIFIC_STATUS` | RW | 0x0 a| -
| 7:4 | `REC_IMG_INDEX` | RW | 0x0 a| -
| 3:0 | `DEV_REC_STATUS` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: Not in recovery mode

* 0x1: Awaiting recovery image

* 0x2: Booting recovery image

* 0x3: Recovery successful

* 0xc: Recovery failed

* 0xd: Recovery image authentication error

* 0xe: Error entering Recovery mode (might be administratively disabled)

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-4-I3C_EC-SecFwRecoveryIf-HW_STATUS]
=== i3c_csr[4].I3C_EC.SecFwRecoveryIf.HW_STATUS

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:24 | `VENDOR_HW_STATUS_LEN` | RW | 0x0 a| 0-251: Length in bytes of Vendor Specific Hardware Status.
| 23:16 | `CTEMP` | RW | 0x0 a| Current temperatureof device in degrees Celsius: Compatible with NVMe-MI command code 0 offset 3.

* 0x00-0x7e: 0 to 126 C

* 0x7f: 127 C or higher

* 0x80: no temperature data, or data is older than 5 seconds

* 0x81: temperature sensor failure

* 0x82-0x83: reserved

* 0xc4: -60 C or lower

* 0xc5-0xff: -59 to -1 C (in two's complement)
| 15:8 | `VENDOR_HW_STATUS` | RW | 0x0 a| -
| 7:3 | `RESERVED_7_3` | RW | 0x0 a| -
| 2 | `FATAL_ERR` | RW | 0x0 a| -
| 1 | `SOFT_ERR` | RW | 0x0 a| Hardware Soft Error (may need reset to clear)
| 0 | `TEMP_CRITICAL` | RW | 0x0 a| Device temperature is critical (may need reset to clear)

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-4-I3C_EC-StdbyCtrlMode-STBY_CR_CONTROL]
=== i3c_csr[4].I3C_EC.StdbyCtrlMode.STBY_CR_CONTROL

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:30 | `STBY_CR_ENABLE_INIT` | RW | 0x0 a| Enables or disables the Secondary Controller:

2'b00 - DISABLED: Secondary Controller is disabled.

2'b01 - ACM_INIT: Secondary Controller is enabled,
but Host Controller initializes in Active Controller mode.

2'b10 - SCM_RUNNING: Secondary Controller operation is enabled,
Host Controller initializes in Standby Controller mode.

2'b11 - SCM_HOT_JOIN: Secondary Controller operation is enabled,
Host Controller conditionally becomes a Hot-Joining Device
to receive its Dynamic Address before operating in Standby Controller mode.
| 29:21 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 20 | `RSTACT_DEFBYTE_02` | RW | 0x0 a| Controls whether I3C Secondary Controller Logic supports RSTACT CCC with
Defining Byte 0x02.

1'b0: NOT_SUPPORTED: Do not ACK Defining Byte 0x02

1'b1: HANDLE_INTR: Support Defining Byte 0x02
| 19:16 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 15 | `DAA_ENTDAA_ENABLE` | RW | 0x0 a| Indicates ENTDAA method is enabled.

1'b0: DISABLED: will not respond

1'b1: ENABLED: will respond
| 14 | `DAA_SETDASA_ENABLE` | RW | 0x0 a| Indicates SETDASA method is enabled.

1'b0: DISABLED: will not respond

1'b1: ENABLED: will respond
| 13 | `DAA_SETAASA_ENABLE` | RW | 0x0 a| Indicates SETAASA method is enabled.

1'b0: DISABLED: will not respond

1'b1: ENABLED: will respond
| 12 | `TARGET_XACT_ENABLE` | RW | 0x1 a| Indicates whether Read-Type/Write-Type transaction servicing is enabled, via
an I3C Target Transaction Interface to software (Section 6.17.3).

1'b0: DISABLED: not available

1'b1: ENABLED: available for software
| 11 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 10:8 | `BAST_CCC_IBI_RING` | RW | 0x0 a| Indicates which Ring Bundle will be used to capture Broadcast CCC data sent by the Active Controller.
The Ring Bundle must be configured and enabled, and its IBI Ring Pair must also be initialized and ready to receive data.
| 7:6 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 5 | `CR_REQUEST_SEND` | W | 0x0 a| Write of 1'b1 to this field shall instruct the Secondary Controller Logic
to attempt to send a Controller Role Request to the I3C Bus.
| 4 | `HANDOFF_DEEP_SLEEP` | RW | 0x0 a| If this field has a value of 1'b1, then the Secondary Controller Logic shall
report a return from Deep Sleep state to the Active Controller.
Writing 1'b1 to this bit is sticky. This field shall automatically clear to 1'b0
after accepting the Controller Role and transitioning to Active Controller mode.

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-4-I3C_EC-StdbyCtrlMode-STBY_CR_VIRTUAL_DEVICE_CHAR]
=== i3c_csr[4].I3C_EC.StdbyCtrlMode.STBY_CR_VIRTUAL_DEVICE_CHAR

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:29 | `BCR_FIXED` | RW | 0x1 a| Bus Characteristics, Fixed Part.

Reset value is set to 3'b001, because this device is an I3C Target,
which supports extended capabilities.

This field is Read-Only while the STBY_CR_ENABLE_INIT field in register
STBY_CR_CONTROL is not zero.
| 28:24 | `BCR_VAR` | RW | 0x10 a| Bus Characteristics, Variable Part.

Reset value is set to 5'b10000, because this device:


- [bit4] is a Virtual Target

- [bit3] is not Offline Capable

- [bit2] does not apply due to bit1

- [bit1] is not capable of IBI requests

- [bit0] has no speed limitation

This field is Read-Only while the STBY_CR_ENABLE_INIT field in register
STBY_CR_CONTROL is not zero.
| 23:16 | `DCR` | RW | 0xBD a| Device Characteristics Register. Value represents an OCP Recovery Device.

This field is Read-Only while the STBY_CR_ENABLE_INIT field in register
STBY_CR_CONTROL is not zero.
| 15:1 | `PID_HI` | RW | 0x7FFF a| High part of the 48-bit Target Device Provisioned ID.

This field is Read-Only while the STBY_CR_ENABLE_INIT field in register
STBY_CR_CONTROL is not zero.
| 0 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
|===

| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31 | `RESET_DYNAMIC_ADDR` | RW | 0x1 a| If set to 1'b1, then the Secondary Controller Logic must clear its Dynamic
Address in register STBY_CR_DEVICE_ADDR after receiving
a Target Reset Pattern that followed a Broadcast or Direct SET RSTACT CCC sent
to the Dynamic Address, with Defining Byte 0x01 or 0x02.
Requires support for Dynamic Address Assignment with at least one supported
method, such as the ENTDAA CCC, with field DAA_ENTDAA_ENABLE set to 1'b1 in
register STBY_CR_CONTROL.
If field ACR_FSM_OP_SELECT in register STBY_CR_CONTROL is set to 1'b1, then
this field shall be cleared (i.e., readiness to accept the Controller Role
shall be revoked) with this Target Reset Pattern.

| 15:8 | `RESET_TIME_PERIPHERAL` | RW | 0x0 a| For Direct GET CCC, this field is returned for Defining Byte 0x81.
| 7:0 | `RST_ACTION` | R | 0x1 a| Contains the Defining Byte received with the last Direct SET CCC sent by the Active Controller. If not armed (START or RSTACT with unsupported defining byte) set to spec default 0x1.
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-4-I3C_EC-TTI-CONTROL]
=== i3c_csr[4].I3C_EC.TTI.CONTROL

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:16 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 15:13 | `IBI_RETRY_NUM` | RW | 0x0 a| Number of times the Target Device will try to request an IBI before giving up.

Values:

0x0 - Device will never retry.

0x1-0x6 - Device will retry this many times.

0x7 - Device will retry indefinitely until the Active Controller sets
DISINT bit in the DISEC command.
| 12 | `IBI_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable the IBI queue servicing.

Values:

0x0 - Device will not service the IBI queue.

0x1 - Device will send IBI requests onto the bus, if possible.
| 11 | `CRR_EN` | RW | 0x0 a| Enable Controller Role Request.

Values:

0x0 - Device is allowed to perform Controller Role Request.

0x1 - Device is not allowed to perform Controller Role Request.
| 10 | `HJ_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable Hot-Join capability.

Values:

0x0 - Device is allowed to attempt Hot-Join.

0x1 - Device is not allowed to attempt Hot-Join.
| 9:0 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-4-I3C_EC-TTI-STATUS]
=== i3c_csr[4].I3C_EC.TTI.STATUS

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:15 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 14:12 | `LAST_IBI_STATUS` | R | 0x0 a| Status of last IBI. Should be read after IBI_DONE interrupt.

Values:

000 - Success: IBI was transmitted and ACK'd by the Active Controller.
001 - FailureNack: Active Controller NACK'd the IBI before any data was sent.
The device will retry sending the IBI as long as permitted by the retry counter.
010 - FailurePartialData: Active Controller NACK'd the IBI after data was
partially sent. The remaining part of data in the IBI queue is considered
corrupted and will be discarded.
011 - FailureRetry: IBI could not be serviced due to reached retry count.
100 - FailureArbitration:: IBI could not be serviced as arbitration was lost
during the address header. The device will retry sending the IBI as long as
permitted by the retry counter.
| 11:9 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 8 | `PROTOCOL_ERROR` | R | 0x0 a| Protocol error occurred in the past. This field can only be reset
by the Controller, if it issues the GETSTATUS CCC.

Values:

0 - no error occurred

1 - generic protocol error occurred in the past. It will be set until reception
of the next GETSTATUS command.
| 7:0 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-4-I3C_EC-TTI-INTERRUPT_STATUS]
=== i3c_csr[4].I3C_EC.TTI.INTERRUPT_STATUS

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31 | `TRANSFER_ERR_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| NOT IMPLEMENTED. Bus error occurred. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 30:27 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 26 | `TX_DESC_COMPLETE` | RW | 0x0 a| Read Transaction on the I3C Bus completed. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 25 | `TRANSFER_ABORT_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| NOT IMPLEMENTED. Bus aborted transaction. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 24:21 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 20 | `PENDING_IBI` | R | 0x0 a| At least one IBI is pending. Read-only, hardware-driven level status: it tracks the live IBI-pending condition and cannot be cleared by software (clears automatically when no IBI is pending).
| 19:16 | `PENDING_INTERRUPT` | RW | 0x0 a| Contains the interrupt number of any pending interrupt, or 0 if no interrupts are pending. This encoding allows for up to 15 numbered interrupts. If more than one interrupt is set, then the highest priority interrupt shall be returned. Read-only, hardware-driven level status: it cannot be cleared by software and updates as pending conditions change.
| 15:14 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 13 | `IBI_DONE` | RW | 0x0 a| IBI is done, check LAST_IBI_STATUS for result. Clear: auto-cleared by hardware when software reads STATUS.LAST_IBI_STATUS; writing 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS also clears it.
| 12 | `IBI_THLD_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| TTI IBI Buffer Threshold Status, the Target Controller shall set this bit to 1 when the number of available entries in the TTI IBI Queue is >= the value defined in `TTI_IBI_THLD`. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 11 | `RX_DESC_THLD_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| TTI RX Descriptor Buffer Threshold Status, the Target Controller shall set this bit to 1 when the number of available entries in the TTI RX Descriptor Queue is >= the value defined in `TTI_RX_DESC_THLD`. Clear: auto-cleared by hardware when software reads the RX Descriptor Queue; writing 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS also clears it.
| 10 | `TX_DESC_THLD_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| TTI TX Descriptor Buffer Threshold Status, the Target Controller shall set this bit to 1 when the number of available entries in the TTI TX Descriptor Queue is >= the value defined in `TTI_TX_DESC_THLD`. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 9 | `RX_DATA_THLD_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| TTI RX Data Buffer Threshold Status, the Target Controller shall set this bit to 1 when the number of entries in the TTI RX Data Queue is >= the value defined in `TTI_RX_DATA_THLD`. Clear: auto-cleared by hardware when software reads the RX Data Queue; writing 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS also clears it.
| 8 | `TX_DATA_THLD_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| TTI TX Data Buffer Threshold Status, the Target Controller shall set this bit to 1 when the number of available entries in the TTI TX Data Queue is >= the value defined in `TTI_TX_DATA_THLD`. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 7:4 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 3 | `TX_DESC_TIMEOUT` | RW | 0x0 a| NOT IMPLEMENTED. Pending Read was NACK’ed, because the `TX_DESC_STAT` event was not handled in time. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 2 | `RX_DESC_TIMEOUT` | RW | 0x0 a| NOT IMPLEMENTED. Pending Write was NACK’ed, because the `RX_DESC_STAT` event was not handled in time. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 1 | `TX_DESC_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| There is a pending Read Transaction on the I3C Bus. Software should write data to the TX Descriptor Queue and the TX Data Queue. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear — writing to the TX queues does not clear it).
| 0 | `RX_DESC_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| There is a pending Write Transaction. Software should read data from the RX Descriptor Queue and the RX Data Queue. Clear: auto-cleared by hardware when software reads the RX Descriptor Queue; writing 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS also clears it.
|===

| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description

| 12 | `RI_INDIRECT_FIFO_OVERFLOW_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable Recovery Interface INDIRECT FIFO overflow error detection and transition to Error state. Set to 0 to disable (overflow is still recorded in interrupt status).
| 11 | `RI_RX_FIFO_OVERFLOW_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable Recovery Interface RX FIFO overflow error detection and transition to Error state. Set to 0 to disable (overflow is still recorded in interrupt status).

| 9 | `RI_READONLY_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable Recovery Interface write-to-read-only error detection. Set to 0 to disable.
| 8 | `RI_LENGTH_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable Recovery Interface length error detection. Set to 0 to disable.
| 7 | `RI_PEC_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable Recovery Interface PEC/CRC error detection. Set to 0 to disable.
| 6 | `FRAMING_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable framing error detection. Set to 0 to disable.
| 5 | `TE5_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable TE5 error detection. Set to 0 to disable.
| 4 | `TE4_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable TE4 error detection. Set to 0 to disable.
| 3 | `TE3_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable TE3 error detection. Set to 0 to disable.
| 2 | `TE2_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable TE2 error detection. Set to 0 to disable.
| 1 | `TE1_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable TE1 error detection. Set to 0 to disable.
| 0 | `TE0_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable TE0 error detection. Set to 0 to disable.
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-4-I3C_EC-TTI-DATA_BUFFER_THLD_CTRL]

| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description

| 26:24 | `RX_START_THLD` | RW | 0x1 a| Minimum number of TTI RX Data queue entries of data received, in DWORDs, that will trigger the TTI RX Data interrupt. Interrupt triggers when `2^(N+1)` RX Buffer DWORD entries are received during the Read transfer.
| 23:19 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 18:16 | `TX_START_THLD` | RW | 0x1 a| Minimum number of available TTI TX Data queue entries, in DWORDs, that will trigger the TTI TX Data interrupt. Interrupt triggers when `2^(N+1)` TX Buffer DWORD entries are available.
| 15:11 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 10:8 | `RX_DATA_THLD` | RW | 0x1 a| Minimum number of TTI RX Data queue entries of data received, in DWORDs, that will trigger the TTI RX Data interrupt. Interrupt triggers when `2^(N+1)` RX Buffer DWORD entries are received during the Read transfer.
| 7:3 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 2:0 | `TX_DATA_THLD` | RW | 0x1 a| Minimum number of available TTI TX Data queue entries, in DWORDs, that will trigger the TTI TX Data interrupt. Interrupt triggers when `2^(N+1)` TX Buffer DWORD entries are available.
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-4-I3C_EC-SoCMgmtIf-SOC_PAD_CONF]
=== i3c_csr[4].I3C_EC.SoCMgmtIf.SOC_PAD_CONF

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:24 | `PAD_TYPE` | RW | 0x1 a| Select pad type

0 - Bidirectional

1 - Open-drain

2 - Input-only

3 - Analog input
| 23:8 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 7 | `VIRTUAL_OD_EN` | RW | 0x0 a| Enable virtual open drain:

0 - disabled

1 - enabled
| 6 | `OD_EN` | RW | 0x0 a| Enable Open-Drain:

0 - disabled

1 - enabled
| 5 | `IO_INVERSION` | RW | 0x0 a| Invert I/O signal:

0 - signals pass-through

1 - signals are inverted
| 4 | `PULL_EN` | RW | 0x0 a| Enable Pull:

0 - disabled

1 - enabled
| 3 | `PULL_DIR` | RW | 0x0 a| Direction of the pull:

0 - Pull down

1 - Pull up
| 2 | `KEEPER_EN` | RW | 0x0 a| Enable the High-Keeper:

0 - disabled

1 - enabled
| 1 | `SCHMITT_EN` | RW | 0x0 a| Enable the Schmitt Trigger:

0 - disabled

1 - enabled
| 0 | `INPUT_ENABLE` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable input:

0 - enabled

1 - disabled
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-5-PIOControl-IBI_PORT]
=== i3c_csr[5].PIOControl.IBI_PORT

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:0 | `IBI_DATA` | R | - a| Data Read from IBI Data Buffer.

The IBI Port is mapped to the IBI Data Queue.
IBI Data is always aligned to a 4-byte boundary and then put into the IBI Queue.
If the incoming data is not aligned to a 4-byte boundary,
then there will be extra (unused) bytes at the end of the transferred IBI data.
This can be determined from the value of field DATA_LENGTH in the IBI Status Descriptor.
|===

| 23:16 | `IBI_DATA_SEGMENT_SIZE` | RW | 0x1 a| IBI Queue data segment size. Valida values are 1:63
| 15:8 | `RESP_BUF_THLD` | RW | 0x1 a| Triggers RESP_READY_STAT interrupt when RESP queue has N or more entries. Accepted values are 1:255
| 7:0 | `CMD_EMPTY_BUF_THLD` | RW | 0x1 a| Triggers CMD_QUEUE_READY_STAT interrupt when CMD queue has N or more free entries. Accepted values are 1:255
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-5-PIOControl-PIO_CONTROL]
=== i3c_csr[5].PIOControl.PIO_CONTROL

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:3 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 2 | `ABORT` | RW | 0x0 a| Stop current command descriptor execution forcefully and hold remaining commands.
1 - Request PIO Abort,
0 - Resume PIO execution
| 1 | `RS` | RW | 0x0 a| Run/Stop execution of enqueued commands.
When set to 0, it holds execution of enqueued commands and runs current command to completion.
1 - PIO Queue start request,
0 - PIO Queue stop request.
| 0 | `ENABLE` | RW | 0x1 a| Enables PIO queues. When disabled, SW may not read from/write to PIO queues.
1 - PIO queue enable request,
0 - PIO queue disable request
|===

| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:16 | `AGENT_CAPS` | RW | 0x0 a| Agent capabilities:

* bit 0: Identification (DEVICE_ID structure)

* bit 1: Forced Recovery (From RESET)

* bit 2: Mgmt reset (From RESET)

* bit 3: Device Reset (From RESET)

* bit 4: Device status (DEVICE_STATUS)

* bit 5: Recovery memory access (INDIRECT_CTRL)

* bit 6: Local C-image support

* bit 7: Push C-image support

* bit 8: Interface isolation

* bit 9: Hardware status

* bit 10: Vendor command

* bit 11: Flashless boot (From RESET)

* bit 12: FIFO CMS support (INDIRECT_FIFO_CTRL)

* bits 13-15: Reserved
| 15:0 | `REC_PROT_VERSION` | RW | 0x0 a| * Byte 0: Major version number = 0x1

* Byte 1: Minor version number = 0x1

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-5-I3C_EC-SecFwRecoveryIf-DEVICE_STATUS_0]
=== i3c_csr[5].I3C_EC.SecFwRecoveryIf.DEVICE_STATUS_0

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:16 | `REC_REASON_CODE` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: No Boot Failure detected (BFNF)

* 0x1: Generic hardware error (BFGHWE)

* 0x2: Generic hardware soft error (BFGSE) - soft error may be recoverable

* 0x3: Self-test failure (BFSTF) (e.g., RSA self test failure, FIPs self test failure,, etc.)

* 0x4: Corrupted/missing critical data (BFCD)

* 0x5: Missing/corrupt key manifest (BFKMMC)

* 0x6: Authentication Failure on key manifest (BFKMAF)

* 0x7: Anti-rollback failure on key manifest (BFKIAR)

* 0x8: Missing/corrupt boot loader (first mutable code) firmware image (BFFIMC)

* 0x9: Authentication failure on boot loader ( 1st mutable code) firmware image (BFFIAF)

* 0xA: Anti-rollback failure boot loader (1st mutable code) firmware image (BFFIAR)

* 0xB: Missing/corrupt main/management firmware image (BFMFMC)

* 0xC: Authentication Failure main/management firmware image (BFMFAF)

* 0xD: Anti-rollback Failure main/management firmware image (BFMFAR)

* 0xE: Missing/corrupt recovery firmware (BFRFMC)

* 0xF: Authentication Failure recovery firmware (BFRFAF)

* 0x10: Anti-rollback Failure on recovery firmware (BFRFAR)

* 0x11: Forced Recovery (FR)

* 0x12: Flashless/Streaming Boot (FSB)

* 0x13-0x7F: Reserved

* 0x80-0xFF: Vendor Unique Boot Failure Codes
| 15:8 | `PROT_ERROR` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: No Protocol Error

* 0x1: Unsupported/Write Command - command is not support or a write to a RO command

* 0x2: Unsupported Parameter

* 0x3: Length write error (length of write command is incorrect)

* 0x4: CRC Error (if supported)

* 0x5-0xFE: Reserved

* 0xFF: General Protocol Error - catch all unclassified errors
| 7:0 | `DEV_STATUS` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: Status Pending (Recover Reason Code not populated)

* 0x1: Device healthy (Recover Reason Code not populated)

* 0x2: Device Error (“soft” error or other error state) - (Recover Reason Code not populated)

* 0x3: Recovery mode - ready to accept recovery image - (Recover Reason Code populated)

* 0x4: Recovery Pending (waiting for activation) - (Recover Reason Code populated)

* 0x5: Running Recovery Image ( Recover Reason Code not populated)

* 0x6-0xD: Reserved

* 0xE: Boot Failure (Recover Reason Code populated)

* 0xF: Fatal Error (Recover Reason Code not populated)

* 0x10-FF:Reserved
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-5-I3C_EC-SecFwRecoveryIf-DEVICE_STATUS_1]

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:25 | `VENDOR_STATUS` | RW | 0x0 a| -
| 24:16 | `VENDOR_STATUS_LENGTH` | RW | 0x0 a| 0-248: Length in bytes of just VENDOR_STATUS. Zero indicates no vendor status and zero additional bytes.
| 15:0 | `HEARTBEAT` | RW | 0x0 a| 0-4095: Incrementing number (counter wraps)
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-5-I3C_EC-SecFwRecoveryIf-DEVICE_RESET]
=== i3c_csr[5].I3C_EC.SecFwRecoveryIf.DEVICE_RESET

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:24 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 23:16 | `IF_CTRL` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: Disable Interface mastering

* 0x1: Enable Interface mastering
| 15:8 | `FORCED_RECOVERY` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: No forced recovery

* 0x01-0xD: Reserved

* 0xE: Enter flashless boot mode on next platform reset

* 0xF: Enter recovery mode on next platform reset

* 0x10-FF: Reserved
| 7:0 | `RESET_CTRL` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: No reset

* 0x1: Reset Device (PCIe Fundamental Reset or equivalent. This is likely bus disruptive)

* 0x2: Reset Management. This reset will reset the management subsystem. If supported, this reset MUST not be bus disruptive (cause re-enumeration)

* 0x3-FF: Reserved
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-5-I3C_EC-SecFwRecoveryIf-RECOVERY_CTRL]
=== i3c_csr[5].I3C_EC.SecFwRecoveryIf.RECOVERY_CTRL

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:24 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 23:16 | `ACTIVATE_REC_IMG` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: do not activate recovery image - after activation device will report this code.

* 0xF: Activate recovery image

* 0x10-FF-reserved
| 15:8 | `REC_IMG_SEL` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: No operation

* 0x1: Use Recovery Image from memory window (CMS)

* 0x2: Use Recovery Image stored on device (C-image)

* 0x3-FF: reserved
| 7:0 | `CMS` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0-255: Selects a component memory space where the recovery image is. 0 is the default
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-5-I3C_EC-SecFwRecoveryIf-RECOVERY_STATUS]
=== i3c_csr[5].I3C_EC.SecFwRecoveryIf.RECOVERY_STATUS

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:16 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 15:8 | `VENDOR_SPECIFIC_STATUS` | RW | 0x0 a| -
| 7:4 | `REC_IMG_INDEX` | RW | 0x0 a| -
| 3:0 | `DEV_REC_STATUS` | RW | 0x0 a| * 0x0: Not in recovery mode

* 0x1: Awaiting recovery image

* 0x2: Booting recovery image

* 0x3: Recovery successful

* 0xc: Recovery failed

* 0xd: Recovery image authentication error

* 0xe: Error entering Recovery mode (might be administratively disabled)

* 0xf: Invalid component address space

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-5-I3C_EC-SecFwRecoveryIf-HW_STATUS]
=== i3c_csr[5].I3C_EC.SecFwRecoveryIf.HW_STATUS

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:24 | `VENDOR_HW_STATUS_LEN` | RW | 0x0 a| 0-251: Length in bytes of Vendor Specific Hardware Status.
| 23:16 | `CTEMP` | RW | 0x0 a| Current temperatureof device in degrees Celsius: Compatible with NVMe-MI command code 0 offset 3.

* 0x00-0x7e: 0 to 126 C

* 0x7f: 127 C or higher

* 0x80: no temperature data, or data is older than 5 seconds

* 0x81: temperature sensor failure

* 0x82-0x83: reserved

* 0xc4: -60 C or lower

* 0xc5-0xff: -59 to -1 C (in two's complement)
| 15:8 | `VENDOR_HW_STATUS` | RW | 0x0 a| -
| 7:3 | `RESERVED_7_3` | RW | 0x0 a| -
| 2 | `FATAL_ERR` | RW | 0x0 a| -
| 1 | `SOFT_ERR` | RW | 0x0 a| Hardware Soft Error (may need reset to clear)
| 0 | `TEMP_CRITICAL` | RW | 0x0 a| Device temperature is critical (may need reset to clear)

Component Memory Space (CMS):

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-5-I3C_EC-StdbyCtrlMode-STBY_CR_CONTROL]
=== i3c_csr[5].I3C_EC.StdbyCtrlMode.STBY_CR_CONTROL

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:30 | `STBY_CR_ENABLE_INIT` | RW | 0x0 a| Enables or disables the Secondary Controller:

2'b00 - DISABLED: Secondary Controller is disabled.

2'b01 - ACM_INIT: Secondary Controller is enabled,
but Host Controller initializes in Active Controller mode.

2'b10 - SCM_RUNNING: Secondary Controller operation is enabled,
Host Controller initializes in Standby Controller mode.

2'b11 - SCM_HOT_JOIN: Secondary Controller operation is enabled,
Host Controller conditionally becomes a Hot-Joining Device
to receive its Dynamic Address before operating in Standby Controller mode.
| 29:21 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 20 | `RSTACT_DEFBYTE_02` | RW | 0x0 a| Controls whether I3C Secondary Controller Logic supports RSTACT CCC with
Defining Byte 0x02.

1'b0: NOT_SUPPORTED: Do not ACK Defining Byte 0x02

1'b1: HANDLE_INTR: Support Defining Byte 0x02
| 19:16 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 15 | `DAA_ENTDAA_ENABLE` | RW | 0x0 a| Indicates ENTDAA method is enabled.

1'b0: DISABLED: will not respond

1'b1: ENABLED: will respond
| 14 | `DAA_SETDASA_ENABLE` | RW | 0x0 a| Indicates SETDASA method is enabled.

1'b0: DISABLED: will not respond

1'b1: ENABLED: will respond
| 13 | `DAA_SETAASA_ENABLE` | RW | 0x0 a| Indicates SETAASA method is enabled.

1'b0: DISABLED: will not respond

1'b1: ENABLED: will respond
| 12 | `TARGET_XACT_ENABLE` | RW | 0x1 a| Indicates whether Read-Type/Write-Type transaction servicing is enabled, via
an I3C Target Transaction Interface to software (Section 6.17.3).

1'b0: DISABLED: not available

1'b1: ENABLED: available for software
| 11 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 10:8 | `BAST_CCC_IBI_RING` | RW | 0x0 a| Indicates which Ring Bundle will be used to capture Broadcast CCC data sent by the Active Controller.
The Ring Bundle must be configured and enabled, and its IBI Ring Pair must also be initialized and ready to receive data.
| 7:6 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 5 | `CR_REQUEST_SEND` | W | 0x0 a| Write of 1'b1 to this field shall instruct the Secondary Controller Logic
to attempt to send a Controller Role Request to the I3C Bus.
| 4 | `HANDOFF_DEEP_SLEEP` | RW | 0x0 a| If this field has a value of 1'b1, then the Secondary Controller Logic shall
report a return from Deep Sleep state to the Active Controller.
Writing 1'b1 to this bit is sticky. This field shall automatically clear to 1'b0
after accepting the Controller Role and transitioning to Active Controller mode.
| 3 | `PRIME_ACCEPT_GETACCCR` | RW | 0x0 a| -
| 2 | `ACR_FSM_OP_SELECT` | RW | 0x0 a| -
| 1 | `HANDOFF_DELAY_NACK` | RW | 0x0 a| -
| 0 | `PENDING_RX_NACK` | RW | 0x0 a| -
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-5-I3C_EC-StdbyCtrlMode-STBY_CR_VIRTUAL_DEVICE_CHAR]
=== i3c_csr[5].I3C_EC.StdbyCtrlMode.STBY_CR_VIRTUAL_DEVICE_CHAR

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:29 | `BCR_FIXED` | RW | 0x1 a| Bus Characteristics, Fixed Part.

Reset value is set to 3'b001, because this device is an I3C Target,
which supports extended capabilities.

This field is Read-Only while the STBY_CR_ENABLE_INIT field in register
STBY_CR_CONTROL is not zero.
| 28:24 | `BCR_VAR` | RW | 0x10 a| Bus Characteristics, Variable Part.

Reset value is set to 5'b10000, because this device:


- [bit4] is a Virtual Target

- [bit3] is not Offline Capable

- [bit2] does not apply due to bit1

- [bit1] is not capable of IBI requests

- [bit0] has no speed limitation

This field is Read-Only while the STBY_CR_ENABLE_INIT field in register
STBY_CR_CONTROL is not zero.
| 23:16 | `DCR` | RW | 0xBD a| Device Characteristics Register. Value represents an OCP Recovery Device.

This field is Read-Only while the STBY_CR_ENABLE_INIT field in register
STBY_CR_CONTROL is not zero.
| 15:1 | `PID_HI` | RW | 0x7FFF a| High part of the 48-bit Target Device Provisioned ID.

This field is Read-Only while the STBY_CR_ENABLE_INIT field in register
STBY_CR_CONTROL is not zero.
| 0 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
|===

Address in register STBY_CR_DEVICE_ADDR after receiving
a Target Reset Pattern that followed a Broadcast or Direct SET RSTACT CCC sent
to the Dynamic Address, with Defining Byte 0x01 or 0x02.
Requires support for Dynamic Address Assignment with at least one supported
method, such as the ENTDAA CCC, with field DAA_ENTDAA_ENABLE set to 1'b1 in
register STBY_CR_CONTROL.
If field ACR_FSM_OP_SELECT in register STBY_CR_CONTROL is set to 1'b1, then
this field shall be cleared (i.e., readiness to accept the Controller Role
shall be revoked) with this Target Reset Pattern.

| 7:0 | `RST_ACTION` | R | 0x1 a| Contains the Defining Byte received with the last Direct SET CCC sent by the Active Controller. If not armed (START or RSTACT with unsupported defining byte) set to spec default 0x1.
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-5-I3C_EC-TTI-CONTROL]
=== i3c_csr[5].I3C_EC.TTI.CONTROL

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:16 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 15:13 | `IBI_RETRY_NUM` | RW | 0x0 a| Number of times the Target Device will try to request an IBI before giving up.

Values:

0x0 - Device will never retry.

0x1-0x6 - Device will retry this many times.

0x7 - Device will retry indefinitely until the Active Controller sets
DISINT bit in the DISEC command.
| 12 | `IBI_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable the IBI queue servicing.

Values:

0x0 - Device will not service the IBI queue.

0x1 - Device will send IBI requests onto the bus, if possible.
| 11 | `CRR_EN` | RW | 0x0 a| Enable Controller Role Request.

Values:

0x0 - Device is allowed to perform Controller Role Request.

0x1 - Device is not allowed to perform Controller Role Request.
| 10 | `HJ_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable Hot-Join capability.

Values:

0x0 - Device is allowed to attempt Hot-Join.

0x1 - Device is not allowed to attempt Hot-Join.
| 9:0 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-5-I3C_EC-TTI-STATUS]
=== i3c_csr[5].I3C_EC.TTI.STATUS

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:15 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 14:12 | `LAST_IBI_STATUS` | R | 0x0 a| Status of last IBI. Should be read after IBI_DONE interrupt.

Values:

000 - Success: IBI was transmitted and ACK'd by the Active Controller.
001 - FailureNack: Active Controller NACK'd the IBI before any data was sent.
The device will retry sending the IBI as long as permitted by the retry counter.
010 - FailurePartialData: Active Controller NACK'd the IBI after data was
partially sent. The remaining part of data in the IBI queue is considered
corrupted and will be discarded.
011 - FailureRetry: IBI could not be serviced due to reached retry count.
100 - FailureArbitration:: IBI could not be serviced as arbitration was lost
during the address header. The device will retry sending the IBI as long as
permitted by the retry counter.
| 11:9 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 8 | `PROTOCOL_ERROR` | R | 0x0 a| Protocol error occurred in the past. This field can only be reset
by the Controller, if it issues the GETSTATUS CCC.

Values:

0 - no error occurred

1 - generic protocol error occurred in the past. It will be set until reception
of the next GETSTATUS command.
| 7:0 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-5-I3C_EC-TTI-INTERRUPT_STATUS]
=== i3c_csr[5].I3C_EC.TTI.INTERRUPT_STATUS

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31 | `TRANSFER_ERR_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| NOT IMPLEMENTED. Bus error occurred. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 30:27 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 26 | `TX_DESC_COMPLETE` | RW | 0x0 a| Read Transaction on the I3C Bus completed. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 25 | `TRANSFER_ABORT_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| NOT IMPLEMENTED. Bus aborted transaction. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 24:21 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 20 | `PENDING_IBI` | R | 0x0 a| At least one IBI is pending. Read-only, hardware-driven level status: it tracks the live IBI-pending condition and cannot be cleared by software (clears automatically when no IBI is pending).
| 19:16 | `PENDING_INTERRUPT` | RW | 0x0 a| Contains the interrupt number of any pending interrupt, or 0 if no interrupts are pending. This encoding allows for up to 15 numbered interrupts. If more than one interrupt is set, then the highest priority interrupt shall be returned. Read-only, hardware-driven level status: it cannot be cleared by software and updates as pending conditions change.
| 15:14 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 13 | `IBI_DONE` | RW | 0x0 a| IBI is done, check LAST_IBI_STATUS for result. Clear: auto-cleared by hardware when software reads STATUS.LAST_IBI_STATUS; writing 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS also clears it.
| 12 | `IBI_THLD_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| TTI IBI Buffer Threshold Status, the Target Controller shall set this bit to 1 when the number of available entries in the TTI IBI Queue is >= the value defined in `TTI_IBI_THLD`. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 11 | `RX_DESC_THLD_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| TTI RX Descriptor Buffer Threshold Status, the Target Controller shall set this bit to 1 when the number of available entries in the TTI RX Descriptor Queue is >= the value defined in `TTI_RX_DESC_THLD`. Clear: auto-cleared by hardware when software reads the RX Descriptor Queue; writing 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS also clears it.
| 10 | `TX_DESC_THLD_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| TTI TX Descriptor Buffer Threshold Status, the Target Controller shall set this bit to 1 when the number of available entries in the TTI TX Descriptor Queue is >= the value defined in `TTI_TX_DESC_THLD`. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 9 | `RX_DATA_THLD_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| TTI RX Data Buffer Threshold Status, the Target Controller shall set this bit to 1 when the number of entries in the TTI RX Data Queue is >= the value defined in `TTI_RX_DATA_THLD`. Clear: auto-cleared by hardware when software reads the RX Data Queue; writing 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS also clears it.
| 8 | `TX_DATA_THLD_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| TTI TX Data Buffer Threshold Status, the Target Controller shall set this bit to 1 when the number of available entries in the TTI TX Data Queue is >= the value defined in `TTI_TX_DATA_THLD`. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 7:4 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 3 | `TX_DESC_TIMEOUT` | RW | 0x0 a| NOT IMPLEMENTED. Pending Read was NACK’ed, because the `TX_DESC_STAT` event was not handled in time. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 2 | `RX_DESC_TIMEOUT` | RW | 0x0 a| NOT IMPLEMENTED. Pending Write was NACK’ed, because the `RX_DESC_STAT` event was not handled in time. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear).
| 1 | `TX_DESC_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| There is a pending Read Transaction on the I3C Bus. Software should write data to the TX Descriptor Queue and the TX Data Queue. Clear: write 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS (no hardware auto-clear — writing to the TX queues does not clear it).
| 0 | `RX_DESC_STAT` | RW | 0x0 a| There is a pending Write Transaction. Software should read data from the RX Descriptor Queue and the RX Data Queue. Clear: auto-cleared by hardware when software reads the RX Descriptor Queue; writing 1 to this bit in INTERRUPT_STATUS also clears it.
|===

| 12 | `RI_INDIRECT_FIFO_OVERFLOW_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable Recovery Interface INDIRECT FIFO overflow error detection and transition to Error state. Set to 0 to disable (overflow is still recorded in interrupt status).

| 7 | `RI_PEC_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable Recovery Interface PEC/CRC error detection. Set to 0 to disable.
| 6 | `FRAMING_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable framing error detection. Set to 0 to disable.
| 5 | `TE5_ERR_DET_EN` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable TE5 error detection. Set to 0 to disable.

| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description

| 2:0 | `TX_DATA_THLD` | RW | 0x1 a| Minimum number of available TTI TX Data queue entries, in DWORDs, that will trigger the TTI TX Data interrupt. Interrupt triggers when `2^(N+1)` TX Buffer DWORD entries are available.
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-oca_i3c_wrap-i3c_csr-5-I3C_EC-SoCMgmtIf-SOC_PAD_CONF]
=== i3c_csr[5].I3C_EC.SoCMgmtIf.SOC_PAD_CONF

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 31:24 | `PAD_TYPE` | RW | 0x1 a| Select pad type

0 - Bidirectional

1 - Open-drain

2 - Input-only

3 - Analog input
| 23:8 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 7 | `VIRTUAL_OD_EN` | RW | 0x0 a| Enable virtual open drain:

0 - disabled

1 - enabled
| 6 | `OD_EN` | RW | 0x0 a| Enable Open-Drain:

0 - disabled

1 - enabled
| 5 | `IO_INVERSION` | RW | 0x0 a| Invert I/O signal:

0 - signals pass-through

1 - signals are inverted
| 4 | `PULL_EN` | RW | 0x0 a| Enable Pull:

0 - disabled

1 - enabled
| 3 | `PULL_DIR` | RW | 0x0 a| Direction of the pull:

0 - Pull down

1 - Pull up
| 2 | `KEEPER_EN` | RW | 0x0 a| Enable the High-Keeper:

0 - disabled

1 - enabled
| 1 | `SCHMITT_EN` | RW | 0x0 a| Enable the Schmitt Trigger:

0 - disabled

1 - enabled
| 0 | `INPUT_ENABLE` | RW | 0x1 a| Enable input:

0 - enabled

1 - disabled
|===

| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description

| 19:0 | `T_HDR_TIMEOUT` | RW | 0xEA60 a| Timer threshold in clock cycles for the optional HDR error recovery (I3C spec 5.1.10.1.9). If both SCL and SDA stay High for this many clock cycles during a TE0/TE1 error-induced HDR mode, the Target exits HDR mode and returns to Idle. Configure based on System Clock to achieve 60us.
|===
